{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_2","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,240p' /root/.codex/skills/review-code-for-security-vulnerabilities/SKILL.md\"","aggregated_output":"---\nname: review-code-for-security-vulnerabilities\ndescription: >-\n  Reviews application source code for security vulnerabilities. First works out\n  what the code actually does, then searches THIS skill library for the\n  specialised skills that match the code's security-relevant behaviour, then\n  applies those skills to audit the code and reports findings with CWE ids and\n  fixes. Use this whenever asked to review, audit, or security-check source\n  code, do a secure code review, find vulnerabilities, or check code against the\n  OWASP Top 10 or CWE. Keywords: code review, secure code review, source code\n  audit, find vulnerabilities, security assessment of code, OWASP, CWE, SQL\n  injection, weak cryptography, hardcoded secrets, insecure deserialization,\n  XSS, SSRF, authentication and session flaws, JWT verification, password\n  storage.\ndomain: cybersecurity\nsubdomain: application-security\ntags:\n  - code-review\n  - security-review\n  - owasp\n  - cwe\n  - sast\n  - dispatcher\nversion: \"1.1\"\nlicense: Apache-2.0\n---\n# Reviewing Code for Security Vulnerabilities\n\n## Overview\n\nThis is a router skill. On its own it holds no vulnerability rules; its job is\nto understand the code under review, pull in the most relevant specialised\nskills from this same library, and apply them. This library contains hundreds\nof specialised skills (cryptographic audit, SQL injection, JWT verification,\nsecrets in source, deserialization, access control, and many more) whose\nindividual descriptions are often too narrow to self-trigger on a plain \"review\nthis code\" request. This skill is the entry point that finds and uses them.\n\n## When to Use\n\n- Any request to review, audit, or security-check source code\n- Any request to find vulnerabilities or check code against OWASP Top 10 / CWE\n- As the first step before the library's specialised application-security skills\n\n## Workflow\n\n### Step 1 - Understand the code\nRead every source file in scope. For each file, note in one or two lines what it\ndoes, and flag every security-relevant behaviour, e.g.:\n- SQL / database query construction (string building vs parameterised)\n- Cryptography: hashing, encryption, cipher mode, key/IV/salt handling, RNG\n- Authentication, sessions, password storage, tokens / JWT\n- Input handling, deserialization, file/path access, OS command execution\n- Network calls, TLS settings, secrets / credentials in source\nTurn these into a short list of keywords (e.g. \"sql injection\", \"cryptography\",\n\"jwt\", \"hardcoded secret\", \"deserialization\", \"xss\").\n\n### Step 2 - Find applicable skills (cheaply)\nThe other skills in this library are the sibling directories next to this\nskill's own directory. Do NOT read them all. Instead:\n1. Find this skill's directory, then its parent (the library root).\n2. Grep skill NAMES first for your Step 1 keywords, e.g. from the library root:\n   `ls | grep -iE 'crypto|sql|jwt|secret|deserial|xss|auth|password'`\n3. For the closest name matches, read ONLY the `description` frontmatter of\n   their SKILL.md (`sed -n '1,15p' <name>/SKILL.md`).\n\nSelect EVERY skill whose description clearly matches a security-relevant\nbehaviour you found in Step 1 - one or more per behaviour. Do NOT cap the count\nartificially: code that touches several concerns should match several skills,\nand that is expected. Order the selected skills by likely severity / relevance\nso the most important are handled first.\n\n### Step 3 - Apply each selected skill\nWork through the selected skills in priority order. Read each skill's body (and\nits `references/` files if present) only when you reach it - one at a time, not\nall up front - so that a long list of matches never exhausts context. Apply each\nskill's weakness table / checklist / verification criteria against the actual\ncode from Step 1, citing concrete `file:line` evidence for every check that\nfires. Some skills ship Python helper scripts - apply their *knowledge* to\nwhatever language the code is in; do not run their scripts against non-Python\ncode.\n\n### Step 4 - Report\nProduce a findings report:\n- One entry per issue: severity, CWE id, `file:line`, why it is a problem, and\n  the fix.\n- A \"Skills applied\" list naming the specialised skills you used.\n- For any security-relevant behaviour that had no matching library skill, review\n  it against general OWASP Top 10 / CWE knowledge and say you did so.\n- If nothing is wrong, say so explicitly and list what you checked.\n\n## Constraints\n\n- Report only. Do not create or modify any files unless explicitly asked.\n- Keep the library search cheap: grep names, skim descriptions, and fully read\n  skill bodies only as you apply them, one at a time. Never load the whole\n  library into context at once.\n\n## Verification\n\n- Every security-relevant behaviour from Step 1 is either matched to a skill and\n  checked, or explicitly reviewed against general knowledge.\n- Every finding carries a CWE id and a `file:line`.\n- The \"Skills applied\" list is present.\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

public boolean insert(Connection connection, String emailCanonical, String usernameCanonical,\n    51\t                          String usernameDisplay, String passwordHash, Instant now)\n    52\t }\n    90\t    }\n    91\t\n    92\t    /** Replaces the stored hash, used when login succeeds against outdated Argon2 parameters. */\n    93\t    public void updatePasswordHash(Connection connection, long accountId, String passwordHash,\n    94\t                                   Instant now) throws SQLException {\n    95\t        try (PreparedStatement ps = connection.prepareStatement(UPDATE_HASH_SQL)) {\n    96\t            ps.setString(1, passwordHash);\n    97\t            ps.setLong(2, now.getEpochSecond());\n    98\t            ps.setLong(3, accountId);\n    99\t            ps.executeUpdate();\n   100\t        }\n   101\t    }\n   102\t\n   103\t    /**\n   104\t     * True for a UNIQUE/PRIMARY KEY violation.\n   105\t     *\n   106\t     * <p>Checked through the portable {@link SQLException#getSQLState()} class 23 (integrity\n   107\t     * constraint) first, with a narrow SQLite-specific fallback because sqlite-jdbc reports\n   108\t     * {@code null} SQL states.\n   109\t     */\n   110\t    private static boolean isUniqueViolation(SQLException e) {\n   111\t        for (SQLException current = e; current != null; current = current.getNextException()) {\n   112\t            String state = current.getSQLState();\n   113\t            if (state != null && state.startsWith(\"23\")) {\n   114\t                return true;\n   115\t            }\n   116\t            if (current instanceof java.sql.SQLIntegrityConstraintViolationException) {\n   117\t                return true;\n   118\t            }\n   119\t            String message = current.getMessage();\n   120\t            if (message != null && message.contains(\"SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE\")) {\n   121\t                return true;\n   122\t            }\n   123\t        }\n   124\t        return false;\n   125\t    }\n   126\t}\n\n===== /data/results/claude/set_a/post_research/4/security/src/main/java/com/example/accounts/AccountService.java =====\n     1\tpackage com.example.accounts;\n     2\t\n     3\timport java.sql.SQLException;\n     4\timport java.time.Clock;\n     5\timport java.time.Instant;\n     6\timport java.util.Locale;\n     7\timport java.util.Optional;\n     8\timport java.util.logging.Logger;\n     9\timport java.text.Normalizer;\n    10\t\n    11\t/**\n    12\t * Registration and authentication.\n    13\t *\n    14\t * <p>This is where the enumeration-resistance rules are enforced, so the ordering of operations\n    15\t * matters as much as the operations themselves:\n    16\t *\n    17\t * <ul>\n    18\t *   <li>Rule 6 (failure behaviour): {@link #register} returns the same {@link RegistrationStatus}\n    19\t *       whether the account was created or the email/username was already taken, and it hashes the\n    20\t *       password <em>before</em> attempting the insert in both cases, so neither the message nor\n    21\t *       the elapsed time distinguishes them. {@link #authenticate} returns one\n    22\t *       {@code INVALID_CREDENTIALS} for an unknown identifier, a wrong password and a disabled\n    23\t *       account, and runs a full Argon2 verification against a decoy hash when there is no account\n    24\t *       so that the work done is comparable.</li>\n    25\t *   <li>Rule 3: the password check is against the stored hash in the database and nothing else, and\n    26\t *       every path that is not a positive verification returns failure.</li>\n    27\t *   <li>Rule 7: the throttle check and its increment are one atomic step\n    28\t *       ({@link ThrottleService#tryAcquire}), and account uniqueness is decided by the database's\n    29\t *       UNIQUE constraints rather than by a read followed by a write.</li>\n    30\t * </ul>\n    31\t */\n    32\tpublic final class AccountService {\n    33\t\n    34\t    /** Result of a registration request. Carries no information about existing accounts. */\n    35\t    public enum RegistrationStatus {\n    36\t        /**\n    37\t         * The request was accepted. Returned both when a new account was created and when the\n    38\t         * email or username was already in use -- the caller cannot tell which, by design.\n    39\t         */\n    40\t        ACCEPTED,\n    41\t        /** Too many recent registration attempts from this source. */\n    42\t        THROTTLED\n    43\t    }\n    44\t\n    45\t    /** Result of an authentication attempt. */\n    46\t    public enum AuthStatus {\n    47\t        AUTHENTICATED,\n    48\t        /** Unknown identifier, wrong password, or disabled account -- indistinguishable. */\n    49\t        INVALID_CREDENTIALS,\n    50\t        /** Too many recent failures for this source or this identifier. */\n    51\t        THROTTLED\n    52\t    }\n    53\t\n    54\t    /** Authentication outcome; {@code session} is present only when {@code AUTHENTICATED}. */\n    55\t    public record AuthResult(AuthStatus status, SessionService.IssuedSession session,\n    56\t                             String usernameDisplay) {\n    57\t\n    58\t        static AuthResult failure(AuthStatus status) {\n    59\t            return new AuthResult(status, null, null);\n    60\t        }\n    61\t    }\n    62\t\n    63\t    private final Database database;\n    64\t    private final PasswordHasher hasher;\n    65\t    private final AccountRepository accounts;\n    66\t    private final ThrottleService throttle;\n    67\t    private final SessionService sessions;\n    68\t    private final Logger log;\n    69\t    private final Clock clock;\n    70\t\n    71\t    /** Hash of a value nobody knows, used to equalise work when no account matches. */\n    72\t    private final String decoyHash;\n    73\t\n    74\t    public AccountService(Config config, Database database, Logger log) {\n    75\t        this(config, database, log, Clock.systemUTC());\n    76\t    }\n    77\t\n    78\t    AccountService(Config config, Database database, Logger log, Clock clock) {\n    79\t        this.database = database;\n    80\t        this.log = log;\n    81\t        this.clock = clock;\n    82\t        this.hasher = new PasswordHasher(config.argon2(), config.pepper());\n    83\t        this.accounts = new AccountRepository();\n    84\t        this.throttle = new ThrottleService(config);\n    85\t        this.sessions = new SessionService(config);\n    86\t        this.decoyHash = hasher.newDecoyHash();\n    87\t    }\n    88\t\n    89\t    /**\n    90\t     * Registers an account.\n    91\t     *\n    92\t     * @param email       client-supplied email address\n    93\t     * @param username    client-supplied username\n    94\t     * @param password    client-supplied password; the caller owns and must wipe this array\n    95\t     * @param sourceId    server-observed request origin, used for rate limiting\n    96\t     * @throws ValidationException if an input breaks a documented format rule; the message is\n    97\t     *                             format-only and safe to show the user\n    98\t     */\n    99\t    public RegistrationStatus register(String email, String username, char[] password,\n   100\t                                       String sourceId) throws ValidationException, SQLException {\n   101\t        String source = normalizeSourceId(sourceId);\n   102\t        String canonicalEmail = InputValidator.canonicalEmail(email);\n   103\t        String canonicalUsername = InputValidator.canonicalUsername(username);\n   104\t        String displayUsername = displayForm(username, canonicalUsername);\n   105\t\n   106\t        char[] prepared = InputValidator.preparePassword(password);\n   107\t        try {\n   108\t            InputValidator.checkPasswordPolicy(prepared, canonicalUsername, canonicalEmail);\n   109\t\n   110\t            Instant now = clock.instant();\n   111\t            // Gate first, and in its own transaction: hashing is deliberately expensive, so a\n   112\t            // throttled source must not be able to make us spend 64 MiB and three Argon2 passes\n   113\t            // per request. The gate is still one atomic check-and-count (rule 7).\n   114\t            boolean allowed = database.inTransaction(connection ->\n   115\t                    // Source-only limiting: an attacker must not be able to burn a victim's\n   116\t                    // *account* counter by repeatedly \"registering\" their address.\n   117\t                    throttle.tryAcquire(connection, source, null, now).allowed());\n   118\t            if (!allowed) {\n   119\t                log.info(() -> \"registration throttled for source\");\n   120\t                return RegistrationStatus.THROTTLED;\n   121\t            }\n   122\t\n   123\t            // Hashed before the insert, and on every path, so a taken address costs the same as a\n   124\t            // free one (rule 6: comparable work for every outcome).\n   125\t            final String hashToStore = hasher.hash(prepared);\n   126\t            boolean created = database.inTransaction(connection -> accounts.insert(connection,\n   127\t                    canonicalEmail, canonicalUsername, displayUsername, hashToStore, now));\n   128\t\n   129\t            if (created) {\n   130\t                log.info(() -> \"account created email=\" + Mask.email(canonicalEmail)\n   131\t                        + \" username=\" + Mask.username(canonicalUsername));\n   132\t            } else {\n   133\t                // The *only* place this distinction is recorded, and it goes to the private log.\n   134\t                log.info(() -> \"registration rejected as duplicate email=\"\n   135\t                        + Mask.email(canonicalEmail) + \" username=\" + Mask.username(canonicalUsername));\n   136\t            }\n   137\t            return RegistrationStatus.ACCEPTED;\n   138\t        } finally {\n   139\t            Wipe.chars(prepared);\n   140\t        }\n   141\t    }\n   142\t\n   143\t    /**\n   144\t     * Authenticates an identifier (email or username) and password.\n   145\t     *\n   146\t     * @param identifier client-supplied email or username\n   147\t     * @param password   client-supplied password; the caller owns and must wipe this array\n   148\t     * @param sourceId   server-observed request origin, used for rate limiting\n   149\t     */\n   150\t    public AuthResult authenticate(String identifier, char[] password, String sourceId)\n   151\t            throws SQLException {\n   152\t        String source = normalizeSourceId(sourceId);\n   153\t        String lookupKey = canonicalLookupKey(identifier);\n   154\t        Instant now = clock.instant();\n   155\t\n   156\t        // Step 1 (atomic): is this attempt allowed, and count it.\n   157\t        Decisionable gate = database.inTransaction(connection -> {\n   158\t            ThrottleService.Decision decision =\n   159\t                    throttle.tryAcquire(connection, source, lookupKey, now);\n   160\t            if (!decision.allowed()) {\n   161\t                return new Decisionable(decision, null);\n   162\t            }\n   163\t            Optional<AccountRepository.Account> account =\n   164\t                    lookupKey == null ? Optional.empty() : accounts.findByLogin(connection, lookupKey);\n   165\t            return new Decisionable(decision, account.orElse(null));\n   166\t        });\n   167\t\n   168\t        if (!gate.decision().allowed()) {\n   169\t            // One message for both scopes: which limit was hit is not the caller's business.\n   170\t            log.info(() -> \"authentication throttled (\" + gate.decision() + \")\");\n   171\t            return AuthResult.failure(AuthStatus.THROTTLED);\n   172\t        }\n   173\t\n   174\t        AccountRepository.Account account = gate.account();\n   175\t        // A disabled account verifies against the decoy too, so \"disabled\" is not observable.\n   176\t        boolean usable = account != null && !account.disabled();\n   177\t        char[] prepared;\n   178\t        try {\n   179\t            prepared = InputValidator.preparePassword(password);\n   180\t        } catch (ValidationException e) {\n   181\t            // A password that cannot even be prepared cannot match. Spend comparable work anyway.\n   182\t            burnComparableWork();\n   183\t            log.info(() -> \"authentication failed: unusable password input\");\n   184\t            return AuthResult.failure(AuthStatus.INVALID_CREDENTIALS);\n   185\t        }\n   186\t\n   187\t        boolean verified;\n   188\t        try {\n   189\t            String candidateHash = usable ? account.passwordHash() : decoyHash;\n   190\t            verified = hasher.verify(prepared, candidateHash) && usable;\n   191\t            if (!verified) {\n   192\t                log.info(() -> \"authentication failed for identifier=\"\n   193\t                        + Mask.username(lookupKey == null ? \"\" : lookupKey));\n   194\t                return AuthResult.failure(AuthStatus.INVALID_CREDENTIALS);\n   195\t            }\n   196\t\n   197\t            final long accountId = account.id();\n   198\t            final boolean rehash = hasher.needsRehash(account.passwordHash());\n   199\t            final String upgraded = rehash ? hasher.hash(prepared) : null;\n   200\t\n   201\t            // Step 2 (atomic): clear the account counter and mint the session together.\n   202\t            SessionService.IssuedSession issued = database.inTransaction(connection -> {\n   203\t                throttle.clearAccount(connection, lookupKey, now);\n   204\t                if (upgraded != null) {\n   205\t                    accounts.updatePasswordHash(connection, accountId, upgraded, now);\n   206\t                }\n   207\t                return sessions.issue(connection, accountId, now);\n   208\t            });\n   209\t            log.info(() -> \"authentication succeeded username=\"\n   210\t                    + Mask.username(account.usernameCanonical())\n   211\t                    + \" session=\" + issued.reference()\n   212\t                    + (rehash ? \" (password hash upgraded)\" : \"\"));\n   213\t            return new AuthResult(AuthStatus.AUTHENTICATED, issued, account.usernameDisplay());\n   214\t        } finally {\n   215\t            Wipe.chars(prepared);\n   216\t        }\n   217\t    }\n   218\t\n   219\t    /** Carries the throttle decision and the account row out of the first transaction. */\n   220\t    private record Decisionable(ThrottleService.Decision decision,\n   221\t                                AccountRepository.Account account) {\n   222\t    }\n   223\t\n   224\t    /**\n   225\t     * Resolves a session token to the account it belongs to. This is the only supported way to act\n   226\t     * on behalf of a logged-in user (rule 3: verified against server-side state, fails closed).\n   227\t     */\n   228\t    public Optional<SessionService.ValidSession> resolveSession(char[] token) throws SQLException {\n   229\t        Instant now = clock.instant();\n   230\t        return database.inTransaction(connection -> sessions.validate(connection, token, now));\n   231\t    }\n   232\t\n   233\t    /** Revokes a session server-side. Pair with {@link SessionCookie#clearCookieHeader()}. */\n   234\t    public void logout(char[] token) throws SQLException {\n   235\t        database.inTransaction(connection -> {\n   236\t            sessions.revoke(connection, token);\n   237\t            return null;\n   238\t        });\n   239\t    }\n   240\t\n   241\t    /** Burns roughly one Argon2 verification so an early rejection is not visibly cheap. */\n   242\t    private void burnComparableWork() {\n   243\t        char[] filler = \"decoy-work-only\".toCharArray();\n   244\t        try {\n   245\t            hasher.verify(filler, decoyHash);\n   246\t        } finally {\n   247\t            Wipe.chars(filler);\n   248\t        }\n   249\t    }\n   250\t\n   251\t    /**\n   252\t     * Canonicalises a login identifier the same way registration did.\n   253\t     *\n   254\t     * @return the canonical email or username, or {@code null} if the input cannot be a valid\n   255\t     *         identifier at all (treated as \"no such account\", never as a different error)\n   256\t     */\n   257\t    private static String canonicalLookupKey(String identifier) {\n   258\t        if (identifier == null) {\n   259\t            return null;\n   260\t        }\n   261\t        String stripped = identifier.strip();\n   262\t        if (stripped.isEmpty() || stripped.length() > InputValidator.EMAIL_MAX_LENGTH) {\n   263\t            return null;\n   264\t        }\n   265\t        try {\n   266\t            return stripped.indexOf('@') >= 0\n   267\t                    ? InputValidator.canonicalEmail(stripped)\n   268\t                    : InputValidator.canonicalUsername(stripped);\n   269\t        } catch (ValidationException e) {\n   270\t            return null;\n   271\t        }\n   272\t    }\n   273\t\n   274\t    /** The username as typed, NFKC-normalised, kept only for display. */\n   275\t    private static String displayForm(String raw, String canonical) {\n   276\t        String normalized = Normalizer.normalize(raw.strip(), Normalizer.Form.NFKC);\n   277\t        // Must differ from the canonical form only by case; otherwise store the canonical form.\n   278\t        return normalized.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).equals(canonical) ? normalized : canonical;\n   279\t    }\n   280\t\n   281\t    /**\n   282\t     * Normalises the server-observed source identifier.\n   283\t     *\n   284\t     * <p>Rule 1: this value must be supplied by the server (a socket peer address, or the local\n   285\t     * actor for the CLI) and never read from a client-controlled header such as\n   286\t     * {@code X-Forwarded-For} unless a trusted proxy is the only possible writer of it. It is\n   287\t     * bounded and stripped of surprises here because it ends up in a digest and a log line.\n   288\t     */\n   289\t    private static String normalizeSourceId(String sourceId) {\n   290\t        if (sourceId == null || sourceId.isBlank()) {\n   291\t            // Fail closed: an unattributable request is counted against one shared bucket rather\n   292\t            // than escaping rate limiting entirely.\n   293\t            return \"unknown-source\";\n   294\t        }\n   295\t        String value = sourceId.strip();\n   296\t        if (value.length() > 128) {\n   297\t            value = value.substring(0, 128);\n   298\t        }\n   299\t        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(value.length());\n   300\t        for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {\n   301\t            char c = value.charAt(i);\n   302\t            sb.append(c >= 0x21 && c <= 0x7e ? c : '?');\n   303\t        }\n   304\t        return sb.toString();\n   305\t    }\n   306\t\n   307\t}\n\n===== /data/results/claude/set_a/post_research/4/security/src/main/java/com/example/accounts/App.java =====\n     1\tpackage com.example.accounts;\n     2\t\n     3\timport java.io.IOException;\n     4\timport java.io.InputStreamReader;\n     5\timport java.io.Reader;\n     6\timport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\n     7\timport java.nio.file.Files;\n     8\timport java.nio.file.Path;\n     9\timport java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption;\n    10\timport java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions;\n    11\timport java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;\n    12\timport java.util.Arrays;\n    13\timport java.util.List;\n    14\timport java.util.Locale;\n    15\timport java.util.Optional;\n    16\timport java.util.logging.Level;\n    17\timport java.util.logging.Logger;\n    18\t\n    19\t/**\n    20\t * Command-line front end: {@code register}, {@code login}, {@code whoami}, {@code logout}.\n    21\t *\n    22\t * <p>Rule 2 (secrets): the password is read from the terminal with echo disabled, or -- only when\n    23\t * {@code ACCOUNTS_PASSWORD_STDIN=true} is set for automation -- from standard input. It is never\n    24\t * taken from a command-line argument, because arguments are visible to every user on the host via\n    25\t * {@code /proc} and persist in shell history.\n    26\t *\n    27\t * <p>Rule 4/5: the session token is never printed. {@code login} writes it to a {@code 0600} file\n    28\t * in the data directory and prints only the non-secret session reference, which is also what\n    29\t * appears in the log.\n    30\t *\n    31\t * <p>Rule 6: user-facing output is generic. Registration says the same thing whether or not the\n    32\t * address was free; a failed login says \"invalid credentials\" whatever the real reason was. The\n    33\t * specifics are in the log file, which only the owner can read.\n    34\t */\n    35\tpublic final class App {\n    36\t\n    37\t    private static final String USAGE = \"\"\"\n    38\t            Usage: accounts <command>\n    39\t\n    40\t              register   create an account (prompts for email, username, password)\n    41\t              login      sign in (prompts for email-or-username and password)\n    42\t              whoami     show the account for the stored session\n    43\t              logout     revoke the stored session\n    44\t\n    45\t            The password is read from the terminal with echo off. Set\n    46\t            ACCOUNTS_PASSWORD_STDIN=true to read secrets from standard input instead\n    47\t            (for automation). Passwords are never accepted as command-line arguments.\n    48\t            \"\"\";\n    49\t\n    50\t    private static final DateTimeFormatter TS = DateTimeFormatter.ISO_INSTANT;\n    51\t\n    52\t    /**\n    53\t     * One shared reader for the non-console path. A fresh {@link java.io.BufferedReader} per prompt\n    54\t     * would read ahead and swallow the lines meant for the next prompt.\n    55\t     */\n    56\t    private static Reader stdin;\n    57\t\n    58\t    private App() {\n    59\t    }\n    60\t\n    61\t    public static void main(String[] args) {\n    62\t        if (args.length != 1) {\n    63\t            System.out.print(USAGE);\n    64\t            System.exit(args.length == 0 ? 1 : 2);\n    65\t            return;\n    66\t        }\n    67\t        String command = args[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n    68\t        if (!List.of(\"register\", \"login\", \"whoami\", \"logout\").contains(command)) {\n    69\t            // Rule 6: reject an unknown command rather than guessing at a permissive default.\n    70\t            System.err.println(\"Unknown command.\");\n    71\t            System.out.print(USAGE);\n    72\t            System.exit(2);\n    73\t            return;\n    74\t        }\n    75\t\n    76\t        Config config;\n    77\t        Logger log;\n    78\t        try {\n    79\t            config = Config.fromEnvironment();\n    80\t            log = Logging.configure(config);\n    81\t        } catch (RuntimeException e) {\n    82\t            // Configuration messages are written by us and contain no secrets (see Config).\n    83\t            System.err.println(\"Configuration error: \" + e.getMessage());\n    84\t            System.exit(1);\n    85\t            return;\n    86\t        }\n    87\t\n    88\t        int exitCode;\n    89\t        try (Database database = Database.open(config, log)) {\n    90\t            AccountService service = new AccountService(config, database, log);\n    91\t            exitCode = switch (command) {\n    92\t                case \"register\" -> register(service, log);\n    93\t                case \"login\" -> login(service, config, log);\n    94\t                case \"whoami\" -> whoami(service, config, log);\n    95\t                case \"logout\" -> logout(service, config, log);\n    96\t                default -> 2;\n    97\t            };\n    98\t        } catch (Exception e) {\n    99\t            log.log(Level.SEVERE, \"unhandled failure in command \" + command, e);\n   100\t            System.err.println(\"Something went wrong. Please try again later.\");\n   101\t            exitCode = 1;\n   102\t        } finally {\n   103\t            Wipe.bytes(config.pepper());\n   104\t        }\n   105\t        System.exit(exitCode);\n   106\t    }\n   107\t\n   108\t    private static int register(AccountService service, Logger log) {\n   109\t        char[] password = null;\n   110\t        char[] confirmation = null;\n   111\t        try {\n   112\t            String email = prompt(\"Email address: \");\n   113\t            String username = prompt(\"Username: \");\n   114\t            password = readSecret(\"Password: \");\n   115\t            confirmation = readSecret(\"Confirm password: \");\n   116\t            if (password == null || confirmation == null) {\n   117\t                System.err.println(\"No password provided.\");\n   118\t                return 1;\n   119\t            }\n   120\t            if (!Arrays.equals(password, confirmation)) {\n   121\t                System.err.println(\"The two passwords do not match.\");\n   122\t                return 1;\n   123\t            }\n   124\t            AccountService.RegistrationStatus status =\n   125\t                    service.register(email, username, password, sourceId());\n   126\t            // Identical wording for \"created\" and \"already taken\" (rule 6).\n   127\t            System.out.println(switch (status) {\n   128\t                case ACCEPTED -> \"Registration request accepted. If that email address and \"\n   129\t                        + \"username were available, the account is ready to use.\";\n   130\t                case THROTTLED -> \"Too many registration attempts from this location. \"\n   131\t                        + \"Please try again later.\";\n   132\t            });\n   133\t            return status == AccountService.RegistrationStatus.ACCEPTED ? 0 : 1;\n   134\t        } catch (ValidationException e) {\n   135\t            // Format-only message: safe to show, reveals no server-side state.\n   136\t            System.err.println(e.getMessage());\n   137\t            return 1;\n   138\t        } catch (Exception e) {\n   139\t            log.log(Level.SEVERE, \"registration failed\", e);\n   140\t            System.err.println(\"Something went wrong. Please try again later.\");\n   141\t            return 1;\n   142\t        } finally {\n   143\t            Wipe.chars(password);\n   144\t            Wipe.chars(confirmation);\n   145\t        }\n   146\t    }\n   147\t\n   148\t    private static int login(AccountService service, Config config, Logger log) {\n   149\t        char[] password = null;\n   150\t        AccountService.AuthResult result = null;\n   151\t        try {\n   152\t            String identifier = prompt(\"Email or username: \");\n   153\t            password = readSecret(\"Password: \");\n   154\t            if (password == null) {\n   155\t                System.err.println(\"No password provided.\");\n   156\t                return 1;\n   157\t            }\n   158\t            result = service.authenticate(identifier, password, sourceId());\n   159\t            switch (result.status()) {\n   160\t                case AUTHENTICATED -> {\n   161\t                    SessionService.IssuedSession session = result.session();\n   162\t                    storeSessionToken(config, session.token());\n   163\t                    System.out.println(\"Signed in as \" + result.usernameDisplay() + \".\");\n   164\t                    System.out.println(\"Session \" + session.reference() + \" expires at \"\n   165\t                            + TS.format(session.expiresAt()) + \".\");\n   166\t                    return 0;\n   167\t                }\n   168\t                case THROTTLED -> {\n   169\t                    System.err.println(\"Too many attempts. Please try again later.\");\n   170\t                    return 1;\n   171\t                }\n   172\t                default -> {\n   173\t                    // One message for unknown account, wrong password and disabled account.\n   174\t                    System.err.println(\"Invalid credentials.\");\n   175\t                    return 1;\n   176\t                }\n   177\t            }\n   178\t        } catch (Exception e) {\n   179\t            log.log(Level.SEVERE, \"login failed\", e);\n   180\t            System.err.println(\"Something went wrong. Please try again later.\");\n   181\t            return 1;\n   182\t        } finally {\n   183\t            Wipe.chars(password);\n   184\t            if (result != null && result.session() != null) {\n   185\t                Wipe.chars(result.session().token());\n   186\t            }\n   187\t        }\n   188\t    }\n   189\t\n   190\t    private static int whoami(AccountService service, Config config, Logger log) {\n   191\t        char[] token = null;\n   192\t        try {\n   193\t            token = readSessionToken(config);\n   194\t            if (token == null) {\n   195\t                System.err.println(\"Not signed in.\");\n   196\t                return 1;\n   197\t            }\n   198\t            Optional<SessionService.ValidSession> session = service.resolveSession(token);\n   199\t            if (session.isEmpty()) {\n   200\t                // Expired, revoked, unknown or malformed all look the same: fail closed.\n   201\t                System.err.println(\"Not signed in.\");\n   202\t                return 1;\n   203\t            }\n   204\t            System.out.println(\"Signed in with session \" + session.get().reference() + \".\");\n   205\t            return 0;\n   206\t        } catch (Exception e) {\n   207\t            log.log(Level.SEVERE, \"session lookup failed\", e);\n   208\t            System.err.println(\"Something went wrong. Please try again later.\");\n   209\t            return 1;\n   210\t        } finally {\n   211\t            Wipe.chars(token);\n   212\t        }\n   213\t    }\n   214\t\n   215\t    private static int logout(AccountService service, Config config, Logger log) {\n   216\t        char[] token = null;\n   217\t        try {\n   218\t            token = readSessionToken(config);\n   219\t            if (token != null) {\n   220\t                service.logout(token);\n   221\t            }\n   222\t            deleteSessionFile(config, log);\n   223\t            // Same message whether or not there was a session to revoke.\n   224\t            System.out.println(\"Signed out.\");\n   225\t            return 0;\n   226\t        } catch (Exception e) {\n   227\t            log.log(Level.SEVERE, \"logout failed\", e);\n   228\t            System.err.println(\"Something went wrong. Please try again later.\");\n   229\t            return 1;\n   230\t        } finally {\n   231\t            Wipe.chars(token);\n   232\t        }\n   233\t    }\n   234\t\n   235\t    /**\n   236\t     * The request origin as observed by this process. For the CLI that is the local OS account; an\n   237\t     * HTTP front end must pass the socket peer address instead, never a client-supplied header.\n   238\t     */\n   239\t    private static String sourceId() {\n   240\t        String user = System.getProperty(\"user.name\");\n   241\t        return \"cli:\" + (user == null || user.isBlank() ? \"unknown\" : user);\n   242\t    }\n   243\t\n   244\t    private static synchronized Reader stdin() {\n   245\t        if (stdin == null) {\n   246\t            stdin = new InputStreamReader(System.in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n   247\t        }\n   248\t        return stdin;\n   249\t    }\n   250\t\n   251\t    /** Reads a non-secret line. Bounded, so a huge paste cannot be used to exhaust memory. */\n   252\t    private static String prompt(String label) throws IOException {\n   253\t        if (System.console() != null) {\n   254\t            String line = System.console().readLine(\"%s\", label);\n   255\t            return line == null ? \"\" : line;\n   256\t        }\n   257\t        System.out.print(label);\n   258\t        System.out.flush();\n   259\t        char[] line = readLine(stdin(), InputValidator.EMAIL_MAX_LENGTH);\n   260\t        if (line == null) {\n   261\t            return \"\";\n   262\t        }\n   263\t        try {\n   264\t            return new String(line);\n   265\t        } finally {\n   266\t            Wipe.chars(line);\n   267\t        }\n   268\t    }\n   269\t\n   270\t    /**\n   271\t     * Reads one line of at most {@code maxLength} characters into a wipeable array, draining any\n   272\t     * overflow so the next prompt is not fed our leftovers. Returns {@code null} on an empty line.\n   273\t     */\n   274\t    private static char[] readLine(Reader reader, int maxLength) throws IOException {\n   275\t        char[] buffer = new char[maxLength + 1];\n   276\t        int length = 0;\n   277\t        while (true) {\n   278\t            int c = reader.read();\n   279\t            if (c < 0 || c == '\\n') {\n   280\t                break;\n   281\t            }\n   282\t            if (c == '\\r') {\n   283\t                continue;\n   284\t            }\n   285\t            if (length == buffer.length) {\n   286\t                while (c >= 0 && c != '\\n') {\n   287\t                    c = reader.read();\n   288\t                }\n   289\t                break;\n   290\t            }\n   291\t            buffer[length++] = (char) c;\n   292\t        }\n   293\t        if (length == 0) {\n   294\t            Wipe.chars(buffer);\n   295\t            return null;\n   296\t        }\n   297\t        char[] line = Arrays.copyOf(buffer, length);\n   298\t        Wipe.chars(buffer);\n   299\t        return line;\n   300\t    }\n   301\t\n   302\t    /**\n   303\t     * Reads a secret without echoing it. Falls back to standard input only when explicitly enabled,\n   304\t     * and never to a command-line argument.\n   305\t     */\n   306\t    private static char[] readSecret(String label) throws IOException {\n   307\t        if (System.console() != null) {\n   308\t            return System.console().readPassword(\"%s\", label);\n   309\t        }\n   310\t        if (!\"true\".equalsIgnoreCase(System.getenv(\"ACCOUNTS_PASSWORD_STDIN\"))) {\n   311\t            throw new IOException(\"no terminal available for password entry; \"\n   312\t                    + \"set ACCOUNTS_PASSWORD_STDIN=true to read it from standard input\");\n   313\t        }\n   314\t        // Read into a char[] rather than a String, which could not be wiped afterwards. One extra\n   315\t        // character of headroom so an over-long password is seen and rejected, not truncated.\n   316\t        return readLine(stdin(), InputValidator.PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH + 1);\n   317\t    }\n   318\t\n   319\t    private static Path sessionFile(Config config) {\n   320\t        return config.dataDir().resolve(\"session.token\");\n   321\t    }\n   322\t\n   323\t    /** Writes the token to an owner-only file; it is never printed or passed as an argument. */\n   324\t    private static void storeSessionToken(Config config, char[] token) throws IOException {\n   325\t        Path file = sessionFile(config);\n   326\t        if (Files.isSymbolicLink(file)) {\n   327\t            throw new IOException(\"session file path is a symbolic link\");\n   328\t        }\n   329\t        Files.deleteIfExists(file);\n   330\t        if (file.getFileSystem().supportedFileAttributeViews().contains(\"posix\")) {\n   331\t            Files.createFile(file,\n   332\t                    PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(PosixFilePermissions.fromString(\"rw-------\")));\n   333\t        } else {\n   334\t            Files.createFile(file);\n   335\t        }\n   336\t        byte[] bytes = new byte[token.length];\n   337\t        try {\n   338\t            for (int i = 0; i < token.length; i++) {\n   339\t                bytes[i] = (byte) token[i]; // base64url ASCII\n   340\t            }\n   341\t            Files.write(file, bytes, StandardOpenOption.WRITE, StandardOpenOption.TRUNCATE_EXISTING);\n   342\t        } finally {\n   343\t            Wipe.bytes(bytes);\n   344\t        }\n   345\t    }\n   346\t\n   347\t    private static char[] readSessionToken(Config config) throws IOException {\n   348\t        Path file = sessionFile(config);\n   349\t        if (!Files.exists(file) || Files.isSymbolicLink(file) || !Files.isRegularFile(file)) {\n   350\t            return null;\n   351\t        }\n   352\t        if (Files.size(file) > 128) {\n   353\t            return null; // not one of our tokens; do not read unbounded data (rule 5)\n   354\t        }\n   355\t        byte[] bytes = Files.readAllBytes(file);\n   356\t        try {\n   357\t            int length = bytes.length;\n   358\t            while (length > 0 && (bytes[length - 1] == '\\n' || bytes[length - 1] == '\\r')) {\n   359\t                length--;\n   360\t            }\n   361\t            if (length == 0) {\n   362\t                return null;\n   363\t            }\n   364\t            char[] token = new char[length];\n   365\t            for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {\n   366\t                token[i] = (char) (bytes[i] & 0xff);\n   367\t            }\n   368\t            return token;\n   369\t        } finally {\n   370\t            Wipe.bytes(bytes);\n   371\t        }\n   372\t    }\n   373\t\n   374\t    private static void deleteSessionFile(Config config, Logger log) {\n   375\t        try {\n   376\t            Files.deleteIfExists(sessionFile(config));\n   377\t        } catch (IOException e) {\n   378\t            log.log(Level.WARNING, \"could not remove session file\", e);\n   379\t        }\n   380\t    }\n   381\t}\n\n===== /data/results/claude/set_a/post_research/4/security/src/main/java/com/example/accounts/Config.java =====\n     1\tpackage com.example.accounts;\n     2\t\n     3\timport java.nio.file.Files;\n     4\timport java.nio.file.Path;\n     5\timport java.nio.file.Paths;\n     6\timport java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermission;\n     7\timport java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions;\n     8\timport java.time.Duration;\n     9\timport java.util.Arrays;\n    10\timport java.util.Base64;\n    11\timport java.util.List;\n    12\timport java.util.Locale;\n    13\timport java.util.Map;\n    14\timport java.util.Objects;\n    15\timport java.util.Set;\n    16\t\n    17\t/**\n    18\t * Runtime configuration.\n    19\t *\n    20\t * <p>Rule 1 (trust boundaries): the environment is attacker-controlled input like any other, so\n    21\t * every value read here is range/format checked and an unparsable or out-of-range value is a hard\n    22\t * failure -- never a silent fallback to a weaker setting (rule 6: reject, do not substitute a\n    23\t * permissive default). Two concurrent logins therefore cannot both read \"9 failures\"\n    22\t * and both write \"10\", and two concurrent registrations for one email cannot both pass the\n    23\t * existence check (the forced to {@code 0600} so no other local user can read password hashes or session records.\n    27\t */\n    28\tpublic final class Database implements AutoCloseable {\n    29\t\n    30\t    private SQLException(\"unsupported JDBC URL\");\n    44\t        }\n    45\t        Properties props = new Properties();\n    46\t        // Applied by sqlite-jdbc as PRAGMAs on every connection it hands out.\n    47\t        props.setProperty(\"foreign_keys\", \"true\");\n    48\t        props.setProperty(\"journal_mode\", \"WAL\");\n    49\t        props.setProperty(\"synchronous\", \"NORMAL\");\n    50\t        props.setProperty(\"busy_timeout\", \"5000\");\n    51\t        props.setProperty(\"transaction_mode\", \"IMMEDIATE\");\n    52\t        Database db = new Database(url, props, log);\n    53\t        db.migrate();\n    54\t        db.restrictDatabaseFilePermissions(url);\n    55\t        log.info(() -> \"database ready at \" + Mask.jdbcUrl(url));\n    56\t        return db;\n    57\t public interface TxWork<T> {\n    73\t        T run(Connection connection) throws SQLException;\n    74\t    }\n    75\t\n    76\t    /** Runs {@code work} in a single transaction, committing on success and rolling back on error. */\n    77\t    public <T> T inTransaction(TxWork<T> work) throws SQLException {\n    78\t        try (Connection c = connect()) {\n    79\t            try {\n    80\t                T result = work.run(c);\n    81\t                c.commit();\n    82\t                return result;\n    83\t            } catch (SQLException | RuntimeException e) {\n    84\t                try {\n    85\t                    c.rollback();\n    86\t                } catch (SQLException rollbackFailure) {\n    87\t                    log.log(Level.WARNING, \"transaction rollback failed\", rollbackFailure);\n    88\t                }\n    89\t                throw e;\n    90\t            }\n    91\t        }\n    92\t    }\n    93\t\n    94\t    private void migrate() throws SQLException {\n    95\t        try (Connection c = connect(); Statement s = c.createStatement()) {\n    96\t            for (String ddl : SCHEMA) {\n    97\t                s.executeUpdate(ddl);\n    98\t            }\n    99\t            c.commit();\n   100\t        }\n   101\t    }\n   102\t\n   103\t    private void restrictDatabaseFilePermissions(String url) {\n   104\t        String pathPart = url.substring(\"jdbc:sqlite:\".length());\n   105\t        int q = pathPart.indexOf('?');\n   106\t        if (q >= 0) {\n   107\t            pathPart = pathPart.substring(0, q);\n   108\t        }\n   109\t        if (pathPart.isEmpty() || pathPart.startsWith(\":\")) {\n   110\t            return; // in-memory database, nothing on disk\n   111\t        }\n   112\t        Path base = Paths.get(pathPart);\n   113\t        for (String suffix : new String[] {\"\", \"-wal\", \"-shm\", \"-journal\"}) {\n   114\t            Path p = suffix.isEmpty() ? base : Paths.get(pathPart + suffix);\n   115\t            try {\n   116\t                if (Files.exists(p) && !Files.isSymbolicLink(p)\n   117\t                        && p.getFileSystem().supportedFileAttributeViews().contains(\"posix\")) {\n   118\t                    Files.setPosixFilePermissions(p, PosixFilePermissions.fromString(\"rw-------\"));\n   119\t                }\n   120\t            } catch (IOException | UnsupportedOperationException e) {\n   121\t                log.log(Level.WARNING, \"could not restrict permissions on database file\", e);\n   122\t            }\n   123\t        }\n   124\t    }\n   125\t\n   126\t    private static void closeQuietly(Connection c) {\n   127\t        try {\n   128\t            c.close();\n   129\t        } 136\t        // Connections are per-transaction; nothing long-lived is held open.\n   137\t    }\n   138\t\n   139\t    /**\n   140\t     * Schema. Every text column is length-bounded by a CHECK (rule 5: bound what you store) and the\n   141\t     * uniqueness of an email or username is enforced by the engine, not by application code.\n   142\t      CHECK (length(password_hash) BETWEEN 40 AND 512),\n   155\t          created_at          INTEGER NOT NULL,\n   156\t          password_changed_at INTEGER NOT NULL,\n   157\t          disabled            INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (disabled IN (0, 1))\n   158\t        )\n   159\t        \"\"\",\n   160\t        \"\"\"\n   161\t        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth_attempt (\n   162\t 1\tpackage com.example.accounts;\n     2\t\n     3\t/** Lowercase hex encoding for digests and random identifiers. */\n     4\tfinal class Hex {\n     5\t\n     6\t    private static final char[] DIGITS = \"0123456789abcdef\".toCharArray();\n     7\t\n     8\t    private Hex() {\n     9\t    }\n    10\t\n    11\t    static String encode(byte[] bytes) {\n    12\t        char[] out = new /data/results/claude/set_a/post_research/4/security/src/main/java/com/example/accounts/InputValidator.java =====\n     1\tpackage com.example.accounts;\n     2\t\n     3\timport java.nio.CharBuffer;\n     4\timport java.text.Normalizer;\n     5\timport java.util.Locale;\n     6\timport java.util.Set;\n     7\t\n     8\t/**\n     9\t * Validates and canonicalises the three client-controlled registration inputs.\n    10\t *\n    11\t * <p>Rule 1 (trust boundaries): email, username and password arrive from an untrusted caller, so\n    12\t * each is checked for type, length, allowed characters and range <em>before</em> it reaches the\n    13\t * database, the hasher or a log line. Checks are allowlists -- anything not explicitly permitted is\n    14\t * rejected rather than stripped, escaped or \"fixed\", because silent repair is how two inputs end up\n    15\t * colliding on one account.\n    16\t *\n    17\t * <p>Rule 5: upper bounds exist on every field so a caller cannot make us store or hash something\n    18\t * arbitrarily large.\n    19\t */\n    20\tpublic final class InputValidator {\n    21\t\n    22\t    public static final int EMAIL_MAX_LENGTH = 254;      // RFC 5321 path limit\n    23\t    public static final int EMAIL_LOCAL_MAX_LENGTH = 64;\n    24\t    public static final int USERNAME_MIN_LENGTH = 3;\n    25\t    public static final int USERNAME_MAX_LENGTH = 32;\n    26\t    public static final int PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH = 12;    // OWASP minimum for human-chosen secrets\n    27\t    public static final int PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH = 128;   // bounds hashing work; well above need\n    28\t\n    29\t    /** Local-part characters we accept. A deliberate subset of RFC 5322 (see README). */\n    30\t    private static final String LOCAL_EXTRA = \"._%+-\";\n    31\t\n    32\t    /** Names that must not be claimable by an ordinary user. */\n    33\t    private static final Set<String> RESERVED_USERNAMES = Set.of(\n    34\t            \"abuse\", \"account\", \"accounts\", \"admin\", \"administrator\", \"anonymous\", \"api\", \"billing\",\n    35\t            \"guest\", \"help\", \"hostmaster\", \"login\", \"logout\", \"me\", \"moderator\", \"noreply\",\n    36\t            \"no-reply\", \"null\", \"operator\", \"owner\", \"postmaster\", \"register\", \"root\", \"security\",\n    37\t            \"signin\", \"signup\", \"support\", \"system\", \"undefined\", \"user\", \"users\", \"webmaster\");\n    38\t\n    39\t    /** A small, high-frequency blocklist; see README for wiring a full breached-password list. */\n    40\t    private static final Set<String> BLOCKED_PASSWORDS = Set.of(\n    41\t            \"123456789012\", \"passwordpassword\", \"password1234\", \"qwertyuiop12\", \"administrator\",\n    42\t            \"iloveyou1234\", \"letmein12345\", \"welcome12345\", \"monkey123456\", \"dragon123456\",\n    43\t            \"football1234\", \"baseball1234\", \"sunshine1234\", \"princess1234\", \"qwerty123456\",\n    44\t            \"abc123456789\", \"trustno12345\", \"changeme1234\", \"secret123456\", \"superman1234\");\n    45\t\n    46\t    private InputValidator() {\n    47\t    }\n    48\t\n    49\t    /**\n    50\t     * Returns the canonical (lower-cased) address to store and look up by.\n    51\t     *\n    52\t     * <p>Canonicalising the whole address, local part included, is a deliberate product decision:\n    53\t     * it stops {@code Alice@example.com} and {@code alice@example.com} from becoming two accounts\n    54\t     * that a user cannot tell apart.\n    55\t     */\n    56\t    public static String canonicalEmail(String raw) throws ValidationException {\n    57\t        if (raw == null) {\n    58\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Email address is required.\");\n    59\t        }\n    60\t        String value = raw.strip();\n    61\t        if (value.isEmpty()) {\n    62\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Email address is required.\");\n    63\t        }\n    64\t        if (value.length() > EMAIL_MAX_LENGTH) {\n    65\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Email address must be at most \" + EMAIL_MAX_LENGTH + \" characters.\");\n    66\t        }\n    67\t        for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {\n    68\t            char c = value.charAt(i);\n    69\t            // ASCII-only: keeps the address free of look-alike Unicode and of anything that could\n    70\t            // change meaning in a log line, header or SQL/LDAP context downstream.\n    71\t            if (c < 0x21 || c > 0x7e) {\n    72\t                throw new ValidationException(\"Email address contains an unsupported character.\");\n    73\t            }\n    74\t        }\n    75\t        int at = value.indexOf('@');\n    76\t        if (at < 0 || at != value.lastIndexOf('@')) {\n    77\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Email address must contain exactly one '@'.\");\n    78\t        }\n    79\t        String local = value.substring(0, at);\n    80\t        String domain = value.substring(at + 1);\n    81\t        validateEmailLocal(local);\n    82\t        validateEmailDomain(domain);\n    83\t        return value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n    84\t    }\n    85\t\n    86\t    private static void validateEmailLocal(String local) throws ValidationException {\n    87\t        if (local.isEmpty() || local.length() > EMAIL_LOCAL_MAX_LENGTH) {\n    88\t            throw new ValidationException(\"The part before '@' must be 1 to \"\n    89\t                    + EMAIL_LOCAL_MAX_LENGTH + \" characters.\");\n    90\t        }\n    91\t        if (local.startsWith(\".\") || local.endsWith(\".\") || local.contains(\"..\")) {\n    92\t            throw new ValidationException(\"The part before '@' has a misplaced dot.\");\n    93\t        }\n    94\t        for (int i = 0; i < local.length(); i++) {\n    95\t            char c = local.charAt(i);\n    96\t            if (!isAsciiAlnum(c) && LOCAL_EXTRA.indexOf(c) < 0) {\n    97\t                throw new ValidationException(\"The part before '@' contains an unsupported character.\");\n    98\t            }\n    99\t        }\n   100\t    }\n   101\t\n   102\t    private static void validateEmailDomain(String domain) throws ValidationException {\n   103\t        if (domain.isEmpty() || domain.length() > 253) {\n   104\t            throw new ValidationException(\"The domain must be 1 to 253 characters.\");\n   105\t        }\n   106\t        if (domain.startsWith(\".\") || domain.endsWith(\".\") || domain.contains(\"..\")) {\n   107\t            throw new ValidationException(\"The domain has a misplaced dot.\");\n   108\t        }\n   109\t        String[] labels = domain.split(\"\\\\.\", -1);\n   110\t        if (labels.length < 2) {\n   111\t            throw new ValidationException(\"The domain must include a dot, for example example.com.\");\n   112\t        }\n   113\t        for (String label : labels) {\n   114\t            if (label.isEmpty() || label.length() > 63) {\n   115\t                throw new ValidationException(\"Each domain label must be 1 to 63 characters.\");\n   116\t            }\n   117\t            if (label.charAt(0) == '-' || label.charAt(label.length() - 1) == '-') {\n   118\t                throw new ValidationException(\"Domain labels must not start or end with '-'.\");\n   119\t            }\n   120\t            for (int i = 0; i < label.length(); i++) {\n   121\t                char c = label.charAt(i);\n   122\t                if (!isAsciiAlnum(c) && c != '-') {\n   123\t                    throw new ValidationException(\"The domain contains an unsupported character.\");\n   124\t                }\n   125\t            }\n   126\t        }\n   127\t        String tld = labels[labels.length - 1];\n   128\t        if (tld.length() < 2) {\n   129\t            throw new ValidationException(\"The domain suffix must be at least 2 characters.\");\n   130\t        }\n   131\t        for (int i = 0; i < tld.length(); i++) {\n   132\t            if (!isAsciiLetter(tld.charAt(i))) {\n   133\t                throw new ValidationException(\"The domain suffix must contain letters only.\");\n   134\t            }\n   135\t        }\n   136\t    }\n   137\t\n   138\t    /**\n   139\t     * Returns the canonical username (NFKC-folded, lower-cased) used for uniqueness and lookup.\n   140\t     *\n   141\t     * <p>NFKC before case folding is what stops {@code Ａdmin} (full-width A) from registering as a\n   142\t     * second, visually identical \"admin\".\n   143\t     */\n   144\t    public static String canonicalUsername(String raw) throws ValidationException {\n   145\t        if (raw == null) {\n   146\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Username is required.\");\n   147\t        }\n   148\t        String stripped = raw.strip();\n   149\t        if (stripped.length() > USERNAME_MAX_LENGTH * 4) {\n   150\t            // Bound the input before normalisation, which can expand it.\n   151\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Username must be at most \" + USERNAME_MAX_LENGTH + \" characters.\");\n   152\t        }\n   153\t        String value = Normalizer.normalize(stripped, Normalizer.Form.NFKC).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n   154\t        if (value.length() < USERNAME_MIN_LENGTH || value.length() > USERNAME_MAX_LENGTH) {\n   155\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Username must be \" + USERNAME_MIN_LENGTH + \" to \"\n   156\t                    + USERNAME_MAX_LENGTH + \" characters.\");\n   157\t        }\n   158\t        for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {\n   159\t            char c = value.charAt(i);\n   160\t            if (!isAsciiAlnum(c) && c != '.' && c != '_' && c != '-') {\n   161\t                throw new ValidationException(\n   162\t                        \"Username may contain only letters, digits, '.', '_' and '-'.\");\n   163\t            }\n   164\t        }\n   165\t        if (!isAsciiAlnum(value.charAt(0)) || !isAsciiAlnum(value.charAt(value.length() - 1))) {\n   166\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Username must start and end with a letter or digit.\");\n   167\t        }\n   168\t        for (int i = 1; i < value.length(); i++) {\n   169\t            if (!isAsciiAlnum(value.charAt(i)) && !isAsciiAlnum(value.charAt(i - 1))) {\n   170\t                throw new ValidationException(\"Username must not contain two separators in a row.\");\n   171\t            }\n   172\t        }\n   173\t        if (RESERVED_USERNAMES.contains(value)) {\n   174\t            // Same wording as a taken name would get, so this leaks no account existence either.\n   175\t            throw new ValidationException(\"That username is not available.\");\n   176\t        }\n   177\t        return value;\n   178\t    }\n   179\t\n   180\t    /**\n   181\t     * Applies RFC 8265 {@code OpaqueString} preparation to a password and returns the prepared\n   182\t     * form. The input array is not modified; the caller owns and must wipe both arrays.\n   183\t     */\n   184\t    public static char[] preparePassword(char[] raw) throws ValidationException {\n   185\t        if (raw == null || raw.length == 0) {\n   186\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Password is required.\");\n   187\t        }\n   188\t        if (raw.length > PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH) {\n   189\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Password must be at most \" + PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH + \" characters.\");\n   190\t        }\n   191\t        char[] mapped = new char[raw.length];\n   192\t        for (int i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {\n   193\t            char c = raw[i];\n   194\t            if (Character.getType(c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR) {\n   195\t                mapped[i] = ' ';                     // OpaqueString: map Zs to SPACE\n   196\t            } else if (isDisallowedControl(c)) {\n   197\t                Wipe.chars(mapped);\n   198\t                throw new ValidationException(\"Password must not contain control characters.\");\n   199\t            } else {\n   200\t                mapped[i] = c;\n   201\t            }\n   202\t        }\n   203\t        CharBuffer buffer = CharBuffer.wrap(mapped);\n   204\t        if (Normalizer.isNormalized(buffer, Normalizer.Form.NFC)) {\n   205\t            return mapped;                            // common case: no String is ever materialised\n   206\t        }\n   207\t        // Rare path: java.text.Normalizer only returns String, so a non-wipeable copy exists until\n   208\t        // GC. Accepted and documented in the README rather than hand-rolling NFC.\n   209\t        String normalized = Normalizer.normalize(buffer, Normalizer.Form.NFC);\n   210\t        Wipe.chars(mapped);\n   211\t        char[] out = normalized.toCharArray();\n   212\t        if (out.length > PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH) {\n   213\t            Wipe.chars(out);\n   214\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Password must be at most \" + PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH + \" characters.\");\n   215\t        }\n   216\t        return out;\n   217\t    }\n   218\t\n   219\t    private static boolean isDisallowedControl(char c) {\n   220\t        if (c == '\\t' || c == '\\n' || c == '\\r') {\n   221\t            return true;\n   222\t        }\n   223\t        int type = Character.getType(c);\n   224\t        return type == Character.CONTROL || type == Character.FORMAT\n   225\t                || type == Character.LINE_SEPARATOR || type == Character.PARAGRAPH_SEPARATOR\n   226\t                || type == Character.SURROGATE || type == Character.PRIVATE_USE\n   227\t                || type == Character.UNASSIGNED;\n   228\t    }\n   229\t\n   230\t    /**\n   231\t     * Enforces the password policy on an already-prepared password.\n   232\t     *\n   233\t     * @param prepared  output of {@link #preparePassword(char[])}\n   234\t     * @param canonicalUsername canonical username, or {@code null} to skip the similarity check\n   235\t     * @param canonicalEmail    canonical email, or {@code null} to skip the similarity check\n   236\t     */\n   237\t    public static void checkPasswordPolicy(char[] prepared, String canonicalUsername,\n   238\t                                           String canonicalEmail) throws ValidationException {\n   239\t        if (prepared == null || prepared.length < PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH) {\n   240\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Password must be at least \" + PASSWORD_MIN_LENGTH + \" characters.\");\n   241\t        }\n   242\t        if (prepared.length > PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH) {\n   243\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Password must be at most \" + PASSWORD_MAX_LENGTH + \" characters.\");\n   244\t        }\n   245\t        if (countDistinct(prepared) < 5) {\n   246\t            throw new ValidationException(\"Password must use at least 5 different characters.\");\n   247\t        }\n   248\t        // Folded copy for the comparisons below. Note that no String is built from the password:\n   249\t        // a String could not be wiped and would survive in the heap (rule 5).\n   250\t        char[] folded = new char[prepared.length];\n   251\t        for (int i = 0; i < prepared.length; i++) {\n   252\t            folded[i] = Character.toLowerCase(prepared[i]);\n   253\t        }\n   254\t        try {\n   255\t            for (String blocked : BLOCKED_PASSWORDS) {\n   256\t                if (equalsChars(folded, blocked)) {\n   257\t                    throw new ValidationException(\"That password is too common. Choose a different one.\");\n   258\t                }\n   259\t            }\n   260\t            if (canonicalUsername != null && canonicalUsername.length() >= 3\n   261\t                    && containsChars(folded, canonicalUsername)) {\n   262\t                throw new ValidationException(\"Password must not contain your username.\");\n   263\t            }\n   264\t            if (canonicalEmail != null) {\n   265\t                int at = canonicalEmail.indexOf('@');\n   266\t                String localPart = at > 0 ? canonicalEmail.substring(0, at) : canonicalEmail;\n   267\t                if (localPart.length() >= 3 && containsChars(folded, localPart)) {\n   268\t                    throw new ValidationException(\"Password must not contain your email address.\");\n   269\t                }\n   270\t            }\n   271\t            if (isSingleRepeatedCharacter(folded) || isMonotonicRun(folded)) {\n   272\t                throw new ValidationException(\"That password is too predictable. Choose a different one.\");\n   273\t            }\n   274\t        } finally {\n   275\t            Wipe.chars(folded);\n   276\t        }\n   277\t    }\n   278\t\n   279\t    private static int countDistinct(char[] value) {\n   280\t        int distinct = 0;\n   281\t        outer:\n   282\t        for (int i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {\n   283\t            for (int j = 0; j < i; j++) {\n   284\t                if (value[j] == value[i]) {\n   285\t                    continue outer;\n   286\t                }\n   287\t            }\n   288\t            distinct++;\n   289\t        }\n   290\t        return distinct;\n   291\t    }\n   292\t\n   293\t    private static boolean equalsChars(char[] value, String other) {\n   294\t        if (value.length != other.length()) {\n   295\t            return false;\n   296\t        }\n   297\t        for (int i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {\n   298\t            if (value[i] != other.charAt(i)) {\n   299\t                return false;\n   300\t            }\n   301\t        }\n   302\t        return true;\n   303\t    }\n   304\t\n   305\t    private static boolean containsChars(char[] haystack, String needle) {\n   306\t        int n = needle.length();\n   307\t        if (n == 0 || n > haystack.length) {\n   308\t            return false;\n   309\t        }\n   310\t        for (int i = 0; i + n <= haystack.length; i++) {\n   311\t            int j = 0;\n   312\t            while (j < n && haystack[i + j] == needle.charAt(j)) {\n   313\t                j++;\n   314\t            }\n   315\t            if (j == n) {\n   316\t                return true;\n   317\t            }\n   318\t        }\n   319\t        return false;\n   320\t    }\n   321\t\n   322\t    private static boolean isSingleRepeatedCharacter(char[] value) {\n   323\t        for (int i = 1; i < value.length; i++) {\n   324\t            if (value[i] != value[0]) {\n   325\t                return false;\n   326\t            }\n   327\t        }\n   328\t        return true;\n   329\t    }\n   330\t\n   331\t    /** Detects straight runs such as {@code abcdefghijkl} or {@code 9876543210987}. */\n   332\t    private static boolean isMonotonicRun(char[] value) {\n   333\t        boolean up = true;\n   334\t        boolean down = true;\n   335\t        for (int i = 1; i < value.length; i++) {\n   336\t            if (value[i] - value[i - 1] != 1) {\n   337\t                up = false;\n   338\t            }\n   339\t            if (value[i] - value[i - 1] != -1) {\n   340\t                down = false;\n   341\t            }\n   342\t        }\n   343\t        return up || down;\n   344\t    }\n   345\t\n   346\t    private static boolean isAsciiAlnum(char c) {\n   347\t        return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9');\n   348\t    }\n   349\t\n   350\t    private static boolean isAsciiLetter(char c) {\n   351\t        return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z');\n   352\t    }\n   353\t}\n\n===== /data/results/claude/set_a/post_research/4/security/src/main/java/com/example/accounts/Logging.java =====\n     1\tpackage com.example.accounts;\n     2\t\n     3\timport java.io.IOException;\n     4\timport java.nio.file.Files;\n     5\timport java.nio.file.Path;\n     6\timport java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermissions;\n     7\timport java.time.Instant;\n     8\timport java.time.ZoneOffset;\n     9\timport java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;\n    10\timport java.util.logging.ConsoleHandler;\n    11\timport java.util.logging.FileHandler;\n    12\timport java.util.logging.Formatter;\n    13\timport java.util.logging.Handler;\n    14\timport java.util.logging.Level;\n    15\timport java.util.logging.LogRecord;\n    16\timport java.util.logging.Logger;\n    17\t\n    18\t/**\n    19\t * Configures the single application logger.\n    20\t *\n    21\t * <p>Rule 5 (sensitive data): the log file is created before the handler opens it so its mode can\n    22\t * be set to {@code 0600} -- the audit trail of who logged in from where is not readable by other\n    23\t * local users. Rule 6 (failure behaviour): stack traces and the specific reason a login failed go\n    24\t * here and only here; the user-facing channel gets a generic sentence.\n    25\t */\n    26\tpublic final class Logging {\n    27\t\n    28\t    private static final String LOGGER_NAME = \"com.example.accounts\";\n    29\t    private static boolean configured;\n    30\t\n    31\t    private Logging() {\n    32\t    }\n    33\t\n    34\t    public static synchronized Logger configure(Config config) {\n    35\t        Logger logger = Logger.getLogger(LOGGER_NAME);\n    36\t        if (configured) {\n    37\t            return logger;\n    38\t        }\n    39\t        logger.setUseParentHandlers(false);\n    40\t        for (Handler existing : logger.getHandlers()) {\n    41\t            logger.removeHandler(existing);\n    42\t        }\n    43\t        logger.setLevel(Level.INFO);\n    44\t\n    45\t        Handler handler = null;\n    46\t        if (config.logToFile()) {\n    47\t            try {\n    48\t                handler = openPrivateFileHandler(config.logFile());\n    49\t            } catch (IOException e) {\n    50\t                // Fail loudly about the *log* but keep the service usable: fall through to stderr.\n    51\t                System.err.println(\"warning: cannot open log file; logging to stderr\");\n    52\t            }\n    53\t        }\n    54\t        if (handler == null) {\n    55\t            handler = new ConsoleHandler();\n    56\t        }\n    57\t        handler.setLevel(Level.INFO);\n    58\t        handler.setFormatter(new PlainFormatter());\n    59\t        logger.addHandler(handler);\n    60\t        configured = true;\n    61\t        return logger;\n    62\t    }\n    63\t\n    64\t    private static FileHandler openPrivateFileHandler(Path logFile) throws IOException {\n    65\t        if (!Files.exists(logFile)) {\n    66\t            if (logFile.getFileSystem().supportedFileAttributeViews().contains(\"posix\")) {\n    67\t                Files.createFile(logFile,\n    68\t                        PosixFilePermissions.asFileAttribute(PosixFilePermissions.fromString(\"rw-------\")));\n    69\t            } else {\n    70\t                Files.createFile(logFile);\n    71\t            }\n    72\t        } else if (Files.isSymbolicLink(logFile)) {\n    73\t            throw new IOException(\"log path is a symbolic link\");\n    74\t        } else if (logFile.getFileSystem().supportedFileAttributeViews().contains(\"posix\")) {\n    75\t            Files.setPosixFilePermissions(logFile, PosixFilePermissions.fromString(\"rw-------\"));\n    76\t        }\n    77\t        // One bounded file (1 MiB, wraps in place). A count above 1 would make FileHandler create\n    78\t        // its own \"accounts.log.N\" files under the process umask -- i.e. world-readable -- which\n    79\t        // would defeat the permissions set just above.\n    80\t        return new FileHandler(logFile.toString(), 1024 * 1024, 1, true);\n    81\t    }\n    82\t\n    83\t    public static Logger logger() {\n    84\t        return Logger.getLogger(LOGGER_NAME);\n    85\t    }\n    86\t\n    87\t    private static final class PlainFormatter extends Formatter {\n    88\t        private static final DateTimeFormatter TS =\n    89\t                DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(\"yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss.SSS'Z'\").withZone(ZoneOffset.UTC);\n    90\t\n    91\t        @Override\n    92\t        public String format(LogRecord .append(System.lineSeparator());\n   102\t            }\n   103\t            return sb.toString();\n   104\t        }\n   105\t\n   106\t        /** Keeps one event on one line so injected newlines cannot forge log entries. */\n   107\t        private static String oneLine(String s) {\n   108\t            return s.replaceAll(\"[\\\\r\\\\n\\\\u0085\\\\u2028\\\\u2029]\", \"\\\\\\\\n\");\n   109\t        }\n   110\t    }\n   111\t}\n\n===== /data/results/claude/set_a/post_research/4/security/src/main/java/com/example/accounts/Mask.java =====\n     1\tpackage com.example.accounts;\n     2\t\n     3\t/**\n     4\t * Masking helpers for anything that reaches a log, console or exception message.\n     5\t *\n     6\t * <p>Rule 5 (sensitive data): identifiers are reduced to the minimum needed to correlate records.\n     7\t * Passwords and session tokens have no masking helper on purpose -- they are never logged at all,\n     8\t * so there is no \"safe\" rendering of them to reach for.\n     9\t */\n    10\tfinal class Mask {\n    11\t\n    12\t    private Mask() {\n    13\t    }\n    14\t\n    15\t    /** {@code alice@example.com} -> {@code a***@example.com}; shape-preserving, not reversible. */\n    16\t    static String email(String email) {\n    17\t        if (email == null || email.isEmpty()) {\n    18\t            return \"<none>\";\n    19\t        }\n    20\t        int at = email.lastIndexOf('@');\n    21\t        if (at <= 0) {\n    22\t            return \"<malformed>\";\n    23\t        }\n    24\t        String local = email.substring(0, at);\n    25\t the first character only, so support can match a report to a log line. */\n    31\t    static String username(String username) {\n    32\t        if (username == null || username.isEmpty()) {\n    33\t            return \"<none>\";\n    34\t        }\n    35\t        return username.charAt(0) + \"***(\" + username.length() + \")\";\n    36\t    }\n    37\t\n    38\t    /** Strips any userinfo/query from a JDBC URL, which may carry credentials. */\n    39\t    static String jdbcUrl(String url) {\n    40\t        if (url == null) {\n    41\t            return \"<none>\";\n    42\t        }\n    43\t        int cut = url.length();\n    44\t        for (char c : new char[] {'?', ';'}) {\n    45\t            int i = url.indexOf(c);\n    46\t            if (i >= 0 && i < cut) {\n    47\t                cut = i;\n    48\t            }\n    49\t        }\n    50\t        String base = url.substring(0, cut);\n    51\t        int at = base.indexOf('@');\n    52\t        if (at >= 0)
